Pseudo:
def softmax_from_x(x):
    x = x - x.max()
    e = np.exp(x)
    return e/e.sum()

Each step:
p = softmax(x)
objective smooth/actual
compute grad_x

m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g

x -= x.mean()

x = np.clip(x, -20, 20)

The SOTA description in prompt mentions step functions and LP maximizing ∑ b_j subject to ||f*g||∞ <= M. That is for a related problem? It normalizes f sum sqrt(2n), M=||f*f||∞. def fft_conv_square(p, L=None):
    n=p.size
    if L is None: L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    return c, P, L
```

Update_best:
```
def actual_score(p):
   p=np.asarray(p); p=np.clip(p,0,1000); n=p.size; s=p.sum()
   if n==0 or s<0.01: return np.inf
   if n <= 1500:
       c=np.convolve(p,p)
   else:
       L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
   maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
   return float(2*n*maxc/(s*s))
```

`normalize_nonneg(u):`
```
u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
u=np.clip(u,0,1e-300)
s=u.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(u.size)/u.size
return u/s
```

`greedy_normalized(n):`
```
a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1

return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

`actual_score_fft(p, direct_limit=2048):` Use L p2.
```
p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size
if n==0: return inf
p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
s=p.sum()
if s<1e-12: return inf
if n <= direct_limit:
    c=np.convolve(p,p)
else:
    L=next_p2(2*n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
return float(2*n*maxc/(s*s))
```
For direct, np.convolve returns full length. Ensure max nonnegative; positive.

`adam_optimize(p, budget_s, seed, lr=None, alpha_start=50, alpha_end=5, name=''):`
- rng optional? For mutations? Maybe not.
- duration = budget_s. If <=0 return p,score.
- n=len(p); L=next_p2(3*n-2)
- x = log(p+eps); x -= mean
- m,v zeros; t=0
- best_p=x? p? best_f=actual_score_fft(p)
- current_f=best_f
- start=time.monotonic()
- if lr None: lr = 0.05 if n<64 else (0.03 if n<256 else 0.02)
- mu=0.9, nu=0.999, eps=1e-8
- progress=0; last_print=0
Loop while time < end:
  t+=1
  alpha = alpha_end + (alpha_start-alpha_end)*np.exp(-3*progress) where progress maybe (time elapsed/duration) not steps. Use `frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/max(1e-6,budget_s))`; alpha = alpha_end + (alpha_start-alpha_end)*np.exp(-4*frac)
  Pg = rfft(p,L)
  c=irfft(Pg*Pg,L)[:2*n-1].real
  c=np.maximum(c,0)
  maxc=c.max()
  if maxc<=0: break
  z = alpha*(c/maxc - 1.0)
  # if alpha large, exp underflow okay
  expz=np.exp(z)
  s=sum(expz)
  if s<=0 or not finite:
      w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[np.argmax(c)]=1
  else: w=expz/s
  W=rfft(w[::-1], L)
  Cfull=irfft(Pg*W,L)[:3*n-2]
  C=Cfull[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
  C=np.maximum(C,0)
  # grad of M wrt p:
  grad_p=4*n*C  # since p sum1
  # ensure finite
  if not finite: break
  gp = np.dot(p,grad_p)
  grad_x = p * (grad_p - gp)
  # optionally add small negative entropy? no
  Adam update:
  m = mu*m + (1-mu)*grad_x
  v = nu*v + (1-nu)*grad_x*grad_x
  mhat=m/(1-mu**t); vhat=v/(1-nu**t)
  x_new = x - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
  # shrink huge
  maxabs=np.max(np.abs(x_new))
  if maxabs <25:
      x = x_new
  else:
      # clip residual
      x = x + np.clip(x_new-x, -5, 5)
  x -= x.mean()
  p = softmax(x)
  # track actual? We have c old. Need new actual for best. Could compute using c? p changed, so not. To avoid extra FFT each step, we can compute actual using C? No, new c not known. We could compute new actual every K steps (e.g., 10 or 50) using actual_score_fft. But best tracking needed. Since we already have Pg/c for old p, not new. If we don't track every step, may miss best. But computing actual every step requires another FFT square. We can combine by computing new c at start of next iteration; then actual old.      new_f = 2*n*maxc  (since p sum1) for current p (old). If new_f < best_f: best. But maxc from c computed. Yes.
  But we compute c before gradient; update best then gradient. Good. No extra cost.
  If time to label.

`try_global_eval(p_list)` with evaluator:
```
def try_global_eval(p_list):
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence(p_list)
       if np.isfinite(val): return float(val)
    except Exception: pass
    return np.inf
```

def scale_output(p, target_sum=10.0):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   q=p*(target_sum/s)
   q=np.clip(q,0,1000)
   return [float(v) for v in q]
```

def lp_improve(p, budget_s, max_iters=20):
    n=len(p)
    if opt is None or n>1200 or budget_s<30: return p, inf
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    f=normalize_nonneg(p)*S0
    # ensure finite
    M=actual_score_fft(f) # since sum S0, score = max(f*f)/S0^2? Wait actual_score_fft(f) returns 2n maxf/fsum^2. fsum=S0. That equals maxf*f? For f sum S0, F=2n*Mf/S0^2=Mf (since S0^2=2n). So actual_score_fft(f)=Mf. Good.
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=M
    end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    # Build A each iteration because f changes.
    iter=0
    while time<end and iter<max_iters:
       if n <= 800 maybe build dense.
       A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), float)
       for k in range(2*n-1): ...
       b=M+1e-10
       cobj=-np.ones(n)
       rem=max(1.0, end-time.monotonic()-1)
       tl=max(5.0, min(60.0, rem/4.0))? For small n, use 20.
       try:
          res=opt.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': tl, 'dual': True, 'presolve': True})
       except TypeError:
          res=opt.linprog(...)
       if res is None or not res.success:
           # maybe try one more with smaller? break
           break
       g=res.x
       if g is None: break
       g=np.asarray(g,float); g=np.clip(g,0,None)
       s=g.sum()
       if s <= 1e-12: break
       g = g * (S0/s)
       # candidate directions
       accepted=None
       for step in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
           fn = (1-step)*f + step*g
           fn = np.clip(fn,0,None)
           # if sum changed due clip? f,g sum S0; clip no effect unless numerical. rescale maybe
           snew=fn.sum()
           if snew <=0: continue
           fn = fn * (S0/snew)
           score = actual_score_fft(fn)  # equals M
           if score < best_score*(1-1e-12):
               accepted=fn; break
       if accepted is None:
           # accept if any step improves current M? Use best_score? above.
           # try step 0.1 no improvement, break or do small 0.01
           step=0.01
           fn=(1-step)*f+step*g; fn=fn*S0/fn.sum()
           score=actual_score_fft(fn)
           if score < best_score:
              accepted=fn
           else:
              break
       f=accepted; M_score=actual_score_fft(f)
       M = float(f*np.sum? no M constraint should be max(f*f) not score? Score=Mf. Use M = max(conv(f,f)). actual_score returns Mf.
       if M_score < best_score:
           best_score=M_score; best_f=f.copy()
       iter+=1
    return normalize_nonneg(best_f), best_score

`get_starts(n, rng)`: return list of p arrays. To limit memory, maybe return generator? Cannot nested. Return list size moderate (<=12). For large n, creating many is okay.
```
def get_starts(n, rng):
   starts=[]
   starts.append(greedy_normalized(n))
   # perturbed greedy
   for sigma in [0.05,0.2,0.5]:
      noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)); p=normalize_nonneg(greedy_normalized(n)*noise); starts.append(p)
   # mix uniform
   for lam in [0.25,0.5,0.75]:
      p=normalize_nonneg(lam*greedy_normalized(n) + (1-lam)*np.ones(n)); starts.append(p)
   # power s
   for s in [0.4,0.6]: starts.append(power_normalized(n,s))
   # random dirichlet
   for alpha in [0.5,1.0,5.0]: starts.append(dirichlet_normalized(n,alpha,rng))
   return starts
```
This is maybe 1+3+3+2+3=12 starts. For each n, optimizing all too much. We'll select subset based on budget.

Let's write functions carefully.

Detailed function code:

`next_power_of_two(m)`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    L=1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L
```

`normalize_nonneg(u)`:
 Ensure 1D. Use `float` conversion. If u scalar? handle.
```
def normalize_nonneg(u):
    a = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        n = a.size if a.size else 16
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)/n
    return a/s
```
Clip at 1000, okay; if a all >1000, normalized sum maybe not original? Evaluator caps at 1000; for internal p scale 1, not issue. If using large f, maybe not.

`greedy_normalized(n)`:
```
def greedy_normalized(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n <=0: n=16
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        a[k] = a[k-1] * (2.0*k - 1.0) / (2.0*k)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

`power_normalized(n, s)`:
```
def power_normalized(n, s):
    n=int(n)
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)+1.0
    a=np.power(idx, -float(s))
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
For s=0.5 same as power, not exact greedy.

`random_start...` top-level:
`def mix_start(n, lam): return normalize_nonneg(lam*greedy_normalized(n)+(1-lam)*np.ones(n))`
`def perturb_greedy(n, rng, sigma): p=greedy_normalized(n); noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)); return normalize_nonneg(p*noise)`
`def dirichlet_start(n, alpha, rng): return normalize_nonneg(rng.dirichlet(alpha*np.ones(n)))` catch exception.

`prepare_start(n, kind, rng)`:
```
def prepare_start(n, kind, rng):
    if kind == 'greedy': return greedy_normalized(n)
    if kind == 'power45': return power_normalized(n,0.45)
    if kind == 'power55': return power_normalized(n,0.55)
    if kind == 'mix25': return normalize_nonneg(0.25*greedy_normalized(n)+0.75*np.ones(n))
    if kind == 'mix50': ...
    if kind == 'mix75': ...
    if kind == 'pert05': return perturb_greedy(n,rng,0.05)
    if kind == 'pert20': ...0.2
    if kind == 'pert50': ...0.5
    if kind == 'dir05': return dirichlet_start(n,0.5,rng)
    if kind == 'dir1': ...
    if kind == 'dir5': ...
    return greedy_normalized(n)
```

`actual_score_fft(p, direct_limit=2048)`:
 Use `np.convolve` for direct. Potential slow for n=2048 direct ~4M ops, okay. Maybe direct_limit=1200 to be safe. Use FFT for 2048. But for evaluator consistency, direct for <=1024? Use 1024. Let's set direct_limit=1024.

`try_global_eval(seq)`: as above.

`scale_output(p, target_sum=10.0)`:
```
def scale_output(p, target_sum=10.0):
   a=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-12:
       return [float(0.0)]*16 # invalid? evaluate inf. Better uniform 16.
   scale = target_sum/s
   if scale <0: scale=0
   b=a*scale
   b=np.clip(b,0.0,1000.0)
   if b.sum() < 0.01:
       b=np.ones(16); b=b*0.01/16? Actually target sum.
   return [float(x) for x in b]
```
If p None, return uniform.

`adam_optimize(p, budget_s, seed=None)`:
 Need rng for random restart? Not. seed maybe for print. Use time.
 Implementation details:
```
def adam_optimize(p, budget_s, seed=0):
    p = np.asarray(p, float).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return p, np.inf
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 0.5:
       f=actual_score_fft(p); return p,f
    x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    x -= x.mean()
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    t=0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_f=actual_score_fft(p)
    if n <64: lr=0.05
    elif n<256: lr=0.03
    elif n<1024: lr=0.02
    else: lr=0.015
    mu=0.9; nu=0.999; eps=1e-8
    L=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max(0.0,budget_s-2.0)
    last_print=start
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       t +=1
       frac = (time.monotonic()-start)/max(1e-9,budget_s)
       alpha = 5.0 + 45.0*np.exp(-4.0*min(1.0, frac*3.0))
       Pg = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
       c = np.fft.irfft(Pg*Pg, L)
       c = c[:2*n-1].real
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
       maxc = float(c.max())
       if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
          break
       f_now = 2.0*n*maxc  # p sum 1
       if np.isfinite(f_now) and f_now < best_f:
          best_f = f_now; best_p = p.copy()
       if time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0:
          print("adam n=%d f=%.6f" % (n, f_now))
          last_print = time.monotonic()
       z = alpha*(c/maxc - 1.0)
       # if z has large negative, exp underflow okay; if large positive? max 0.
       expz = np.exp(z)
       sz = float(expz.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 0.0:
          w = np.zeros(2*n-1); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       else:
          w = expz/sz
       W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
       Cfull = np.fft.irfft(Pg*W, L)
       C = Cfull[n-1:2*n-1][::-1].real
       C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
       grad_p = (4.0*n)*C
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)): break
       gp = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
       grad_x = p*(grad_p - gp)
       # remove mean? no
       m = mu*m + (1.0-mu)*grad_x
       v = nu*v + (1.0-nu)*grad_x*grad_x
       mhat = m/(1.0-mu**t)
       vhat = v/(1.0-nu**t)
       step = lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       # Limit step in x to e.g. 1.0 to avoid divergence
       step = np.clip(step, -2.0, 2.0)
       x = x - step
       x -= x.mean()
       maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
       if not np.isfinite(maxabs) or maxabs > 40.0:
          x = np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)
          x -= x.mean()
       x = x - x.max()
       e = np.exp(x)
       se = float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(se) or se <=0: break
       p = e/se
    final_f = actual_score_fft(p)
    if final_f < best_f:
       best_f=final_f; best_p=p.copy()
    return best_p, best_f
```
Note f_now = 2n maxc because p sum1. p from softmax sum1. Good.
The step clip -2,2 may be too restrictive? Adam step per variable maybe. If too small, slow. Follow Adam, maybe no clip needed except huge. Use clip 5? Let's set `clip_val = 5.0`. But if grad_x has small vhat, step could large; clip 5 okay. Then x recenter and softmax. Could cause slow if always clipped. Maybe `clip_val = 3.0`. We'll use 5.

Potential issue: Print every 10s may clutter but okay. Use print only if f improved? The prompt says output shown; okay maybe prints many. Limit prints to 50? We can print only on improvement or every 60s. Simpler: no print inside to reduce? But useful. We'll print at start maybe. But final answer not executed here. Keep prints limited.

`lp_improve_rows(p, budget_s, max_rows=None)`:
Implement with concurrency? No.
```
def lp_improve_rows(p, budget_s, max_rows=None):
    if opt is None:
       f=actual_score_fft(p); return p,f
    n=p.size
    if n<2: n=16
    if max_rows is None:
       if n > 3000: max_rows=700
       elif n > 1000: max_rows=900
       else: max_rows=1024
    # cap dense memory: max_rows*n <= 8_000_000? If too high reduce
    while max_rows*n > 8_000_000: max_rows = int(max_rows*0.8)
    max_rows = max(max_rows, 64)
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    f=normalize_nonneg(p)*S0
    cur_score=actual_score_fft(f) # M
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    end=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,budget_s-5.0)
    # initial rows
    R0 = int(min(max_rows, max(80, max_rows//2)))
    rows = [int(x) for x in np.unique(np.linspace(0, 2*n-2, R0, dtype=int).astype(int)).tolist()]
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       iter +=1
       # build A
       rows_arr = np.array(rows, dtype=int)
       A = np.zeros((rows_arr.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
       for ii,k in enumerate(rows_arr):
           lo = k - n + 1
           if lo < 0: lo = 0
           hi = k if k < n else n-1
           if lo <= hi:
               # source f[k-hi : k-lo+1] reversed
               A[ii, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
       b = np.full(rows_arr.size, cur_score, dtype=np.float64) # cur_score = max(f*f)
       cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
       rem = max(1.0, end - time.monotonic() - 1.0)
       tl = max(5.0, min(120.0, rem*0.25))
       try:
          res = opt.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': tl})
       except TypeError:
          res = opt.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method='highs')
       except Exception:
          break
       if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
          # If failed due unbounded, add rows? break
          break
       g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
       sg = float(g.sum())
       if sg <= 1e-12:
          break
       # check violations with current f, M=cur_score
       if n > 1024:
          L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
          P=np.fft.rfft(f,L); Q=np.fft.rfft(g,L)
          conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Q, L)[:2*n-1].real
       else:
          conv=np.convolve(f,g)
       conv=np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
       tol = 1e-7 + 1e-7*cur_score
       violations = conv - cur_score
       if np.any(violations > tol):
           # add top violations if room
           if len(rows) < max_rows:
               idx = np.argsort(violations)[::-1]
               add = 0
               row_set = set(rows)
               for idxk in idx:
                  if add >= max_rows - len(rows) or add >= 512: break
                  ix=int(idxk)
                  if violations[ix] > tol and ix not in row_set:
                     rows.append(ix); row_set.add(ix); add +=1
               # continue to resolve with new A without updating f
               continue
       # no violation or no room; accept direction
       dest_rows? no
       g = g * (S0/sg)
       accepted=None; best_step_score=np.inf
       for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.15, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
           fn = (1.0-step)*f + step*g
           fn = np.clip(fn, 0.0, None)
           snew = float(fn.sum())
           if snew <= 1e-12: continue
           fn = fn * (S0/snew)
           score = actual_score_fft(fn)
           if score < best_step_score:
              best_step_score=score; accepted=fn.copy()
       if accepted is None or not np.isfinite(best_step_score):
          break
       if best_step_score < best_score:
          f = accepted; cur_score = best_step_score
          best_f = f.copy(); best_score = cur_score
       else:
          # no improvement, break
          break
       # Optionally print
       if iter % 5 == 0 or time.monotonic() > end-? print
    final_f=actual_score_fft(best_f)
    return normalize_nonneg(best_f), min(final_f,best_score)
```
Note in LP, b should be M = max(f*f), which is `cur_score` for f sum S0. But if we update f, `cur_score` updated. Good.
When adding rows, we `continue` without updating f. We rebuild A at top. Inner could loop; need limit to avoid endless if many violations and max_rows reached. We have if no room, proceed even with violations. Good.

Memory: `row_set` set of ints each add; okay.

Potential issue: `linprog` with dense A and unbounded if rows not covering. Our initial grid spacing maybe too sparse? If max_rows*? For n=6962,max_rows=700? gap ~20, interval length 6962, covered. Good. If max_rows small 64, gap ~218, still <6962. Good.

But if initial grid rows not include a row for every column? For each variable j, interval [j,j+n-1]. If grid spacings max_gap < n, intersects. Since grid over full range with R points, max gap ~ (2n-2)/(R-1). For R>=3, <n if 2n/(R-1)<n => R>3. So yes. Thus bounded.

Could `linprog` fail due to too many variables and `A_ub` dense memory. Wrap in try MemoryError; break.

```
def record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n):
    if p is None: return best_p,best_score
    p=np.asarray(p)
    if p.size==0: return...
    f=actual_score_fft(p)
    if np.isfinite(f) and f < best_score:
       best_p=p.copy(); best_score=f
    n=int(p.size)
    old = best_by_n.get(n)
    if old is None or f < old[0]:
       best_by_n[n]=(f,p.copy())
    return best_p,best_score
```
Top-level function can use np, actual_score. No closure. In main, call:
`best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)`.
`best_by_n` mutated. Good.

But record_candidate recomputes actual score, while we may already have score. Could accept optional `score` to avoid. Define:
```
def record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, score=None):
    if score is None: score=actual_score_fft(p)
    ...
```
Good.

def main_search(seed, budget_s):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf; best_by_n={}
    # baselines
    lengths=[8,12,16,24,32,48,64,96,128,192,256,384,512,768,1024]
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       p=greedy_normalized(n)
       best_p,best_score=record_candidate(p,... )
       p2=power_normalized(n,0.45); update
       p3=power_normalized(n,0.55); update
    # global seq
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if h is not None: ...
    # jobs
    jobs = [...]
    for n,kind,alloc in jobs:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem<10+alloc/4: break? maybe if rem<alloc:
       if kind=='best1024'? We'll handle.
       if kind == 'best':
           base = best_by_n[n][1] if n in best_by_n else greedy_normalized(n)
       else:
           base = prepare_start(n, kind, rng)
       alloc_use = min(float(alloc), max(5.0, rem-5.0))
       p_best, f_best = adam_optimize(base, alloc_use, seed=(seed+n))
       best_p,best_score=record_candidate(p_best,...,f_best)
       if best_score < 1.45: # good, can shorten future? We can reduce alloc.
           pass
```

`random_perturb` top-level:
```
def random_perturb(p, rng, sigma):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if p.size==0: return greedy_normalized(16)
   noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, p.size))
   return normalize_nonneg(p*noise)
```

Now, let's scrutinize `adam_optimize` p sum: At start `p=normalize_nonneg`. Softmax uses x log(p). If p has values extremely small, log(-)? p positive due normalize_nonneg clips to 0 and if some zeros? `normalize_nonneg` with `np.clip(a,0,1e-300)`, so zeros become 1e-300, log finite -690. Good. If all zeros, returns uniform.

Step clipping: `step=np.clip(step, -5,5)`. Could be too small if Adam needs bigger? For high dim, step per variable maybe <1; okay. If p needs strong change, many steps. Maybe use 10? But x step 1 doubles probability; maybe too oscillatory. Use 5 conservative.

Alpha update: we compute alpha based on elapsed each step; okay. If budget_s large, alpha decays. For a 100s job, at 33s alpha=5+45*e^-4=7.3; at 60s ~5. Good.

We may want support `hard_stop` global? `adam_optimize` has end=budget_s-2; if called with alloc_use less than rem, okay. It doesn't know global deadline but alloc derived. Good.

```
if res is None: break
g=res.x

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing

    f(seq) = 2*n*max(convolve(seq, seq)) / sum(seq)^2

using several complementary numerical heuristics:
  1. Fast FFT-based gradient descent with a smoothed maximum objective.
  2. Several randomized/structured initial points and length trials.
  3. A scalable cutting-plane implementation of the previous LP-based
     iteration when SciPy/HiGHS is available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
except Exception:
    _scipy_opt = None


def next_power_of_two(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def normalize_nonneg(u):
    a = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-300, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def greedy_normalized(n):
    """Coefficients of the formal square root of 1/(1-x), normalized."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 16
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def power_normalized(n, s):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 16
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    a = np.power(idx, -float(s))
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def perturb_greedy(n, rng, sigma):
    p = greedy_normalized(n)
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), int(n)))
    return normalize_nonneg(p * noise)


def mix_greedy_uniform(n, lam):
    p = lam * greedy_normalized(n) + (1.0 - lam) * np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def dirichlet_start(n, alpha, rng):
    n = int(n)
    try:
        u = rng.dirichlet(float(alpha) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_nonneg(u)


def random_perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return greedy_normalized(16)
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size))
    return normalize_nonneg(p * noise)


def prepare_start(n, kind, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if kind == "greedy":
        return greedy_normalized(n)
    if kind == "power45":
        return power_normalized(n, 0.45)
    if kind == "power55":
        return power_normalized(n, 0.55)
    if kind == "mix25":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.25)
    if kind == "mix50":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.50)
    if kind == "mix75":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.75)
    if kind == "pert05":
        return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.05)
    if kind == "pert15":
        return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.15)
    if kind == "pert20":
        return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.20)
    if kind == "pert50":
        return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.50)
    if kind == "dir05":
        return dirichlet_start(n, 0.5, rng)
    if kind == "dir1":
        return dirichlet_start(n, 1.0, rng)
    if kind == "dir5":
        return dirichlet_start(n, 5.0, rng)
    return greedy_normalized(n)


def actual_score_fft(p, direct_limit=768):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01 * 1e-6:
        return np.inf

    if n <= direct_limit:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        L = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
        P = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s))


def scale_output(p, target_sum=10.0):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    b = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    bs = float(b.sum())
    if bs < 0.01:
        b = b * (0.01 / bs)
    return [float(x) for x in b]


def try_global_eval(seq_list):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(list(seq_list))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def tuple_score_key(x):
    return x[0]


def record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, score=None):
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_p, best_score
    if score is None:
        score = actual_score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best_p, best_score
    if score < best_score:
        best_p = a.copy()
        best_score = float(score)
    n = int(a.size)
    old = best_by_n.get(n)
    if old is None or score < old[0]:
        best_by_n[n] = (float(score), a.copy())
    return best_p, best_score


def adam_optimize(p, budget_s, seed=0):
    """
    Softmax-projected gradient descent for a smoothed maximum-convolution
    objective.  p is interpreted as sum-one probabilities.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 0.5:
        f = actual_score_fft(p)
        return p, f

    x = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    x -= x.mean()

    if n < 64:
        lr = 0.05
    elif n < 256:
        lr = 0.03
    elif n < 1024:
        lr = 0.02
    else:
        lr = 0.015

    mu, nu, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    L = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_f = actual_score_fft(p)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 2.0)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        t += 1
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, budget_s))
        alpha = 5.0 + 45.0 * np.exp(-4.0 * frac)

        Pg = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Pg * Pg, L)[:2 * n - 1].real
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        f_now = float(2.0 * n * maxc)  # p sums to one
        if np.isfinite(f_now) and f_now < best_f:
            best_f = f_now
            best_p = p.copy()

        z = alpha * (c / maxc - 1.0)
        expz = np.exp(z)
        sz = float(expz.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = expz / sz

        W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
        Cfull = np.fft.irfft(Pg * W, L)
        C = Cfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].real
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)

        grad_p = (4.0 * n) * C
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
            break
        gp = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
        grad_x = p * (grad_p - gp)

        m = mu * m + (1.0 - mu) * grad_x
        v = nu * v + (1.0 - nu) * (grad_x * grad_x)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - mu ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - nu ** t)
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -50.0, 50.0)
        x = x - step
        x -= x.mean()

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break
        x = np.clip(x, -80.0, 80.0)
        x -= x.mean()
        x = x - x.max()
        e = np.exp(x)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            break
        p = e / se

    final_f = actual_score_fft(p)
    if final_f < best_f:
        best_f = final_f
        best_p = p.copy()
    return best_p, best_f


def lp_improve_rows(p, budget_s, max_rows=None):
    """
    Cutting-plane version of the LP-direction method:

      maximize sum(g)
      subject to f*g <= M, g >= 0

    where f is normalized to sum sqrt(2n) and M = max(f*f).
    """
    if _scipy_opt is None:
        f = actual_score_fft(p)
        return p, f

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        n = 16
        p = normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s < 25.0:
        f = actual_score_fft(p)
        return p, f

    if max_rows is None:
        if n > 4000:
            max_rows = 900
        elif n > 1500:
            max_rows = 1000
        else:
            max_rows = 1100

    # Keep dense A within a modest element count.
    max_elements = 6000000
    while max_rows * n > max_elements:
        max_rows = max(128, int(max_rows * 0.85))
    max_rows = max(128, min(int(max_rows), max_elements // max(1, n)))

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    f = normalize_nonneg(p) * S0
    cur_score = actual_score_fft(f)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        return p, cur_score

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(cur_score)

    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s - 5.0)

    r0 = min(max_rows, max(80, max_rows // 2))
    rows0 = np.unique(np.linspace(0, 2 * n - 2, r0, dtype=int).astype(int))
    rows = [int(k) for k in rows0.tolist()]
    row_set = set(rows)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        rows_arr = np.asarray(rows, dtype=int)
        A = np.zeros((int(rows_arr.size), n), dtype=np.float64)
        for ii in range(int(rows_arr.size)):
            k = int(rows_arr[ii])
            lo = k - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = k if k < n else n - 1
            if lo <= hi:
                A[ii, lo:hi + 1] = f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

        b = np.full(int(rows_arr.size), cur_score, dtype=np.float64)
        cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

        rem = max(1.0, end - time.monotonic() - 1.0)
        tl = max(5.0, min(90.0, rem * 0.25))
        res = None
        try:
            res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=(0.0, None),
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(tl)},
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
                    cobj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b,
                    bounds=(0.0, None),
                    method="highs",
                )
            except Exception:
                break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-12:
            break

        # Check full-constraint violations.
        if n > 1024:
            L2 = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
            Pf = np.fft.rfft(f, L2)
            Pg = np.fft.rfft(g, L2)
            conv = np.fft.irfft(Pf * Pg, L2)[:2 * n - 1].real
        else:
            conv = np.convolve(f, g)
        conv = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
        tol = 1e-7 + 1e-7 * cur_score
        violations = conv - cur_score
        add_needed = np.any(violations > tol)

        if add_needed:
            added = 0
            if len(rows) < max_rows:
                order = np.argsort(violations)[::-1]
                for ix in order:
                    if added >= 0.5 * (max_rows - len(rows)) or added >= 256:
                        break
                    ix = int(ix)
                    if violations[ix] > tol and ix not in row_set:
                        rows.append(ix)
                        row_set.add(ix)
                        added += 1
            if added == 0:
                # If existing constraints are still violated too much, stop this local LP phase.
                if float(np.max(violations)) > 10.0 * tol:
                    break
            else:
                continue

        g = g * (S0 / sg)

        accepted = None
        accepted_score = np.inf
        for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02):
            fn = (1.0 - step) * f + step * g
            fn = np.clip(fn, 0.0, None)
            sn = float(fn.sum())
            if sn <= 1e-12:
                continue
            fn = fn * (S0 / sn)
            sc = actual_score_fft(fn)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < accepted_score:
                accepted_score = float(sc)
                accepted = fn.copy()

        if accepted is None or not np.isfinite(accepted_score):
            break
        if accepted_score >= best_score:
            break

        f = accepted
        cur_score = accepted_score
        best_f = f.copy()
        best_score = cur_score

        if it % 5 == 0:
            print("LP cut n=%d iter=%d score=%.6f rows=%d" % (n, it, best_score, len(rows)))

    final_score = actual_score_fft(best_f)
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
    return normalize_nonneg(best_f), best_score


def main_search(seed, budget_s):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_by_n = {}

    # Quick baselines.
    base_lengths = [8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048]
    for n in base_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        p = greedy_normalized(n)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)
        p = power_normalized(n, 0.45)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)
        p = power_normalized(n, 0.55)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)

    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size:
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(arr, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Gradient phases.  The schedule is intentionally conservative but diversified.
    jobs = [
        (64, "greedy", 10),
        (128, "greedy", 18),
        (192, "greedy", 25),
        (256, "greedy", 38),
        (384, "greedy", 40),
        (512, "greedy", 55),
        (768, "greedy", 55),
        (1024, "greedy", 65),
        (1536, "greedy", 50),
        (2048, "greedy", 60),

        (128, "pert05", 16),
        (128, "mix50", 16),
        (128, "power45", 16),
        (256, "pert15", 30),
        (256, "mix75", 30),
        (256, "dir1", 30),
        (256, "power45", 30),
        (384, "pert50", 35),
        (384, "mix50", 35),
        (384, "dir5", 35),
        (512, "pert20", 45),
        (512, "mix75", 45),
        (512, "dir1", 45),
        (768, "pert50", 45),
        (768, "mix50", 45),
        (1024, "best", 70),
        (1536, "pert20", 55),
        (1536, "mix50", 55),
        (2048, "best", 70),
    ]

    for n, kind, alloc in jobs:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 18.0:
            break
        if kind == "best":
            rec = best_by_n.get(int(n))
            base = rec[1] if rec is not None else greedy_normalized(n)
        else:
            base = prepare_start(n, kind, rng)
        alloc_use = min(float(alloc), max(5.0, rem - 5.0))
        p_out, f_out = adam_optimize(base, alloc_use, seed=(int(seed) + int(n)))
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)

    # LP/cutting-plane phase, if available.
    if _scipy_opt is not None:
        lp_candidates = [256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]
        if best_p is not None:
            ln = int(np.asarray(best_p).size)
            if ln <= 8000 and ln not in lp_candidates:
                lp_candidates.append(ln)
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            try:
                ln = int(np.asarray(h).size)
                if ln <= 8000 and ln not in lp_candidates:
                    lp_candidates.append(ln)
            except Exception:
                pass

        lp_scored = []
        for n in lp_candidates:
            rec = best_by_n.get(int(n))
            sc = rec[0] if rec is not None else 2.0
            lp_scored.append((float(sc), int(n)))
        lp_scored.sort(key=tuple_score_key)

        for _, n in lp_scored:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 75.0:
                break
            rec = best_by_n.get(int(n))
            base = rec[1] if rec is not None else greedy_normalized(n)
            alloc = min(240.0, max(55.0, 0.38 * rem))
            p_out, f_out = lp_improve_rows(base, alloc)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)
    else:
        # Fallback: more Adam if LP is unavailable.
        while deadline - time.monotonic() > 90.0:
            if best_p is not None:
                base = random_perturb(best_p, rng, 0.08)
            else:
                base = greedy_normalized(256)
            alloc = min(220.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
            p_out, f_out = adam_optimize(base, alloc, seed=int(seed))
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)
            if best_score < 1.45:
                break

    # Final local polishing.
    if best_p is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            p0 = random_perturb(best_p, rng, 0.07)
            p_out, f_out = adam_optimize(p0, min(rem - 20.0, 240.0), seed=int(seed) + 999)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60.0:
            p0 = normalize_nonneg(best_p)
            p_out, f_out = adam_optimize(p0, min(rem - 5.0, 180.0), seed=int(seed) + 12345)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = greedy_normalized(256)
        best_score = actual_score_fft(best_p)

    # Validate a few candidates against the authoritative evaluator if time permits.
    candidates = []
    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(best_p)
    items = []
    for n, rec in best_by_n.items():
        items.append((rec[0], int(n), rec[1]))
    items.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    for sc, n, p in items[:4]:
        if p is not None:
            candidates.append(p)

    for cand in candidates:
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 15.0:
            break
        try:
            seq = scale_output(cand, 10.0)
            gscore = try_global_eval(seq)
            if np.isfinite(gscore) and gscore < best_score:
                best_score = gscore
                best_p = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        final_seq = scale_output(best_p, 10.0)
    except Exception:
        final_seq = scale_output(greedy_normalized(256), 10.0)

    return final_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Entry point: return a list of non-negative floats."""
    try:
        return main_search(int(seed), float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        p = greedy_normalized(512)
        return scale_output(p, 10.0)
```
